                                                        Strawberry-Rhubarb Jam
Ingredients                           Directions
4 1/4 cups diced rhubarb              Place rhubarb, strawberries, lemon juice, fruit pectin, and butter into
4 1/4 cups sliced fresh               a large kettle over medium heat. (Butter is optional but helps keep
strawberries                          jam from getting too foamy). Stir the fruit mixture to help the juice
2 tablespoons lemon juice             start to form, and add sugar, about 1 cup at a time, stirring
2 (1.75 ounce) packages               constantly until sugar is dissolved and the juice is starting to
powdered fruit pectin                 simmer. Turn up heat to medium-high, bring the mixture to a full
1/2 teaspoon butter (optional)        rolling boil, and cook and stir for 1 minute. Skim off any foam that
                                      forms.
10 cups white sugar
12 half-pint canning jars with lids
                                      Sterilize the jars and lids in boiling water for at least 5 minutes. With
and rings                             a jelly funnel and a soup ladle, pack the jam into the hot, sterilized
                                      jars, filling the jars to within 1/4 inch of the top. Run a knife or a thin
                                      spatula around the insides of the jars after they have been filled to
                                      remove any air bubbles. Wipe the rims of the jars with a moist paper
                                      towel to remove any food residue. Top with lids, and screw on
                                      rings.

                                      Place a rack in the bottom of a large stockpot and fill halfway with
                                      water. Bring to a boil over high heat, then carefully lower the jars
                                      into the pot using a holder. Leave a 2 inch space between the jars.
                                      Pour in more boiling water if necessary until the water level is at
                                      least 1 inch above the tops of the jars. Bring the water to a full boil,
                                      cover the pot, and process for 5 minutes.

                                      Remove the jars from the stockpot and place onto a cloth-covered
                                      or wood surface, several inches apart, until cool. To help the jam
                                      set, don't move or touch the jars until cooled. Once cool, press the
                                      top of each lid with a finger, ensuring that the seal is tight (lid does
                                      not move up or down at all). Store in a cool, dark area.
